# KstyleUI_IconsColour provenance

Icon set B. Every icon in `Icons/` has a row below. `Tools/icons/iconset.py package` writes this file and the icons from the generation records; do not edit it by hand.

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Model | Higgsfield `gpt_image_2_5` |
| Settings | `--aspect_ratio 1:1 --resolution 1k --quality high` |
| Style reference | `docs/design/mockups/icons_B_fullcolour.jpg`, passed as `image_references` |
| Seed | The model exposes none; the Higgsfield job ID identifies each image |
| Post-processing | Pillow: Lanczos downscale from 1024 to 128 pixels, full colour (RGB PNG) |
| Checks | `Tools/icons/check_icons.py --set B`: no blank, flat (unreadable at 32 pixels), framed, grid, off-centre or text-bearing image is accepted |

Each `style` prompt is the row's prompt text followed by this style block:

```text
Paint one bold, iconic subject that captures this ability: the ability's own object, weapon, creature or magical effect, large and centred, filling most of the square, with a strong clear silhouette and one bright focal point, so it still reads when the icon is shrunk to 32 pixels. Show the effect itself rather than a casting hand or a whole character; draw a hand or a figure only when the ability is about one. The painting fills the whole square edge to edge, with a dark, softly blurred background in matching colours and no empty margin; no scene and no landscape. Style: match the reference image: a rich, full-colour fantasy painting like a high-quality MMORPG spell icon, saturated colours, dramatic glowing light, painterly brushwork and fine detail. Keep the subject whole inside the square. One icon only: a single square painting, not a grid, a sheet or a set. No text, no letters, no numbers, no runes, no writing, no border, no frame, no black margin, no rounded corners.
```

## Repainted from Blizzard's icon (`subject_reference`)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Blizzard source | The icon's BLP2 texture, read by file ID from the installed WoW Forever client (`/Applications/World of Warcraft`, product `wowf`) with TACTTool (`iconset.py extract`, read-only), decoded with Pillow, composited over black and upscaled to 512 pixels. The row's sha256 is the BLP's. No Blizzard file is in this repository. |
| Image references | 1: the Blizzard icon (the subject); 2: `docs/design/mockups/icons_B_buttons.png`, the action-button strip (finish only) |
| Checks | `Tools/icons/check_icons.py --set faithful`: no blank, flat (unreadable at 32 pixels), grid or text-bearing image is accepted, no black margin with a hard edge round the painting (a vignette is not a frame), and no repaint whose 8x8 light-and-dark layout correlates below 0.6 with Blizzard's icon (drift) |

Each `subject_reference` prompt is the row's prompt text followed by this style block:

```text
Style: a crisp, clean, high-resolution fantasy ability icon for a modern, minimal, dark game UI. Painterly but clean: smooth confident brushwork, sharp readable edges, strong value contrast, one bright focal point, a dark near-black backdrop with a soft vignette so the subject pops at small sizes. The art fills the whole square edge to edge: no frame, no border, no bevel, no rounded corners, no margin. No text, letters, numbers or runes. One icon only.
```
